                }

                $existing = Product::where('oranos_product_id', $oranosId)->first();
                // All provider prices use one admin-defined global markup.
                // Keep an explicitly disabled product disabled.
                if ($existing && ! $existing->is_active && $existing->oranos_available) {
                    $isActive = false;
                }

                $payload = [
                    'category_id'      => $category->id,
                    'name'             => $name,
                    'name_ar'          => $name,
                    'description'      => $name,
                    'description_ar'   => $name,
                    'base_price'       => $oranosPrice,
                    'price'            => $ourRetail,
                    'pricing_mode'     => 'auto',
                    'is_automation'    => true,
                    'qty_values'       => $qtyValues,
                    'params'           => $params,
                    'is_active'        => $isActive,
                    'oranos_available' => $available,
                    'stock'            => $available ? 999999999 : 0,
                    'metadata'         => ['oranos_product_type' => $product['product_type'] ?? null, 'oranos_category_name' => $categoryName],
                ];

                if ($categoryImage !== null) {
                    // This is category_img, not an independently documented product picture.
                    // Store it as a fallback; the owner's image always wins.
                    $payload['oranos_image_url'] = $categoryImage;
                    if ($category->oranos_image_url !== $categoryImage) {
                        $category->update(['oranos_image_url' => $categoryImage]);
                    }
                }

                if ($existing) {
                    // Never overwrite merchant edits during provider synchronization.
                    foreach (['name_ar', 'description', 'description_ar'] as $merchantField) {
                        unset($payload[$merchantField]);
                    }
                    if (($existing->pricing_mode ?? 'auto') !== 'auto') {
                        unset($payload['price'], $payload['pricing_mode']);
                    }
                    // An explicit admin disable is not provider availability.
                    if (! $existing->is_active && $existing->oranos_available) {
                        $payload['is_active'] = false;
                    }
                    $existing->update($payload);
                    $updated++;
                } else {
                    $slug = Str::slug($name) . '-' . $oranosId;
                    if (empty($slug)) $slug = 'product-' . $oranosId;
                    $base = $slug; $i = 2;
                    while (Product::where('slug', $slug)->exists()) {
                        $slug = $base . '-' . $i++;
                    }

                    $payload['slug'] = $slug;
                    $payload['oranos_product_id'] = $oranosId;
                    $payload['type'] = 'auto';
                    $payload['pricing_mode'] = 'auto';

                    Product::create($payload);
                    $synced++;
                }

                if (! $isActive) $inactive++;
            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                $failed++;
                Log::warning('Failed to sync Oranos product', [
                    'oranos_id' => $product['id'] ?? null,
                    'error' => $e->getMessage(),
                ]);
            }
        }

        $this->newLine();
        $this->info('=== SYNC COMPLETE ===');
        $this->info("New products:      {$synced}");
        $this->info("Updated products:  {$updated}");
        $this->info("Inactive:          {$inactive}");
        $this->info("No category:       {$noCategory}");
        $this->info("Failed:            {$failed}");

        return ($failed > 0 || $noCategory > 0) ? self::FAILURE : self::SUCCESS;
    }

    protected function preloadCategories(): void
    {
        Category::query()->chunk(500, function ($chunk) {
            foreach ($chunk as $cat) {
                if ($cat->oranos_category_id) {
                    $this->categoriesByOranosId[$cat->oranos_category_id] = $cat;
                }
                if ($cat->name) {
                    $this->categoriesByName[$cat->name] = $cat;
                }
                if ($cat->name_ar && $cat->name_ar !== $cat->name) {
                    $this->categoriesByName[$cat->name_ar] = $cat;
                }
            }
        });
    }
}


===== FILE: app\Console\Commands\SyncOranosProgressive.php =====

<?php

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\Category;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Setting;
use App\Services\OranosMarketService;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/**
 * Non-destructive progressive Oranos import.
 * Never deletes products, sends orders, or disables records absent from partial provider responses.
 */
class SyncOranosProgressive extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'oranos:sync-progressive {--max-minutes=45 : Time limit for this run} {--dry-run : Preview without writes}';
    protected $description = 'Import verified Oranos content incrementally with persistent checkpoints and safe price markup';

    public function handle(OranosMarketService $api): int
    {
        $folder = storage_path('app/oranos-progressive');
        if (!is_dir($folder) && !@mkdir($folder, 0755, true) && !is_dir($folder)) {
            $this->error('Cannot create checkpoint directory');
            return self::FAILURE;
        }
        $lock = fopen($folder.'/sync.lock', 'c');
        if (!$lock || !flock($lock, LOCK_EX | LOCK_NB)) {
            $this->warn('SYNC_ALREADY_RUNNING');
            if ($lock) fclose($lock);
            return self::SUCCESS;
        }
        $deadline = microtime(true) + max(2, min(360, (int) $this->option('max-minutes'))) * 60;
        $dry = (bool) $this->option('dry-run');
        $stats = ['fetched' => 0, 'cached' => 0, 'categories_created' => 0, 'products_created' => 0, 'products_updated' => 0, 'invalid_prices' => 0, 'failed' => 0];
        try {
            $markup = (float) Setting::get('oranos_markup_percent', 20);
            if (!is_finite($markup) || $markup < 0 || $markup > 10000) throw new \RuntimeException('Invalid markup percent');
            $queue = [[0, null]];
            $seen = [];
            $state = $folder.'/last-success.json';
            while ($queue && microtime(true) < $deadline) {
                [$providerId, $parentId] = array_shift($queue);
                if (isset($seen[$providerId])) continue;
                $seen[$providerId] = true;
                $cacheFile = $folder.'/category-'.$providerId.'.json';
                $content = null;
                $age = is_file($cacheFile) ? time() - filemtime($cacheFile) : PHP_INT_MAX;
                if ($age >= 0 && $age < 12 * 3600) {
                    $candidate = json_decode((string) file_get_contents($cacheFile), true);
                    if ($this->validContent($candidate)) {
                        $content = $candidate;
                        $stats['cached']++;
                    }
                }
                for ($attempt = 1; $content === null && $attempt <= 3 && microtime(true) < $deadline; $attempt++) {
                    try {
                        $candidate = $api->getContent((int)$providerId);
                        if (!$this->validContent($candidate)) throw new \RuntimeException('Malformed response');
                        $content = $candidate;
                        $encoded = json_encode($content, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
                        if ($encoded === false) throw new \RuntimeException('Cannot serialize provider response');
                        $temp = $cacheFile.'.'.getmypid().'.tmp';
                        if (file_put_contents($temp, $encoded, LOCK_EX) === false || !rename($temp, $cacheFile)) {
                            @unlink($temp);
                            throw new \RuntimeException('Cannot save checkpoint');
                        }
                        $stats['fetched']++;
                    } catch (\Throwable $e) {
                        $this->warn('FETCH_RETRY category='.$providerId.' attempt='.$attempt.' type='.get_class($e));
                        if ($attempt < 3) sleep(min(20, 4 * $attempt));
                    }
                }
                if ($content === null) {
                    $stats['failed']++;
                    // Do not discard a failed node. The next run retries it from the live API.
                    continue;
                }
                $children = [];
                foreach ($content['categories'] as $child) {
                    if (!is_array($child) || !filter_var($child['id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT)) continue;
                    $cid = (int) $child['id'];
                    if ($cid <= 0 || $cid === $providerId) continue;
                    $name = trim((string)($child['name'] ?? ''));
                    if ($name === '' || strtolower($name) === 'null') continue;
                    $children[$cid] = ['name' => $name, 'parent' => $providerId === 0 ? null : $providerId];
                    $queue[] = [$cid, $providerId];
                }
                if ($dry) {
                    $this->line('DRY_NODE '.$providerId.' children='.count($children).' products='.count($content['products']));
                    continue;
                }
                // Commit each fully validated node atomically. Never write an incomplete node.
                DB::transaction(function () use ($providerId, $parentId, $children, $content, $markup, &$stats) {
                    $parent = $providerId === 0 ? null : Category::where('oranos_category_id', $providerId)->first();
                    if ($providerId !== 0 && !$parent) throw new \RuntimeException('Missing parent #'.$providerId);
                    foreach ($children as $cid => $info) {
                        $cat = Category::where('oranos_category_id', $cid)->first();
                        if (!$cat) {
                            $cat = Category::create([
                                'oranos_category_id' => $cid,
                                'parent_id' => $parent?->id,
                                'name' => $info['name'], 'name_ar' => $info['name'],
                                'slug' => 'oranos-cat-'.$cid,
                                'type' => 'auto', 'icon' => 'package', 'sort_order' => 0,
                            ]);
                            $stats['categories_created']++;
                        } elseif ($cat->parent_id !== $parent?->id) {
                            $cat->update(['parent_id' => $parent?->id]);
                        }
                    }
                    foreach ($content['products'] as $data) {
                        if (!is_array($data) || !filter_var($data['id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT)) continue;
                        $pid = (int)$data['id'];
                        if ($pid <= 0) continue;
                        $name = trim((string)($data['name'] ?? ''));
                        if ($name === '') continue;
                        $assignedProviderId = filter_var($data['parent_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT) ?: $providerId;
                        $localCat = Category::where('oranos_category_id', $assignedProviderId)->first();
                        if (!$localCat && $providerId !== 0) $localCat = $parent;
                        if (!$localCat) {
                            $this->warn('UNMAPPED_PRODUCT '.$pid.' category='.$assignedProviderId);
                            continue;
                        }
                        $validPrice = is_numeric($data['price'] ?? null) && is_finite((float)$data['price']) && (float)$data['price'] > 0;
                        $price = $validPrice ? (float)$data['price'] : 0;
                        if (!$validPrice) $stats['invalid_prices']++;
                        $availability = $data['available'] ?? true;
                        if (is_string($availability)) $availability = in_array(strtolower($availability), ['active','available','in_stock','true','1'], true);
                        $availability = (bool)$availability;
                        $existing = Product::where('oranos_product_id', $pid)->first();
                        $payload = [
                            'category_id' => $localCat->id,
                            'base_price' => $price,
                            'oranos_available' => $availability,
                            'qty_values' => $data['qty_values'] ?? null,
                            'params' => is_array($data['params'] ?? null) ? $data['params'] : [],
                        ];
                        if ($existing) {
                            if (($existing->pricing_mode ?? 'auto') === 'auto' && $validPrice) {
                                $payload['price'] = round($price * (1 + $markup / 100), 4);
                            }
                            // Never automatically re-enable a manually disabled product.
                            if ($existing->is_active && (!$availability || !$validPrice)) $payload['is_active'] = false;
                            $existing->update($payload);
                            $stats['products_updated']++;
                        } else {
                            Product::create(array_merge($payload, [
                                'oranos_product_id' => $pid, 'name' => $name, 'name_ar' => $name,
                                'description' => $name, 'description_ar' => $name,
                                'slug' => 'oranos-product-'.$pid, 'type' => 'auto',
                                'is_automation' => true, 'pricing_mode' => 'auto',
                                'price' => $validPrice ? round($price * (1 + $markup / 100), 4) : 0,
                                'stock' => $availability ? 999999999 : 0,
                                'is_active' => $availability && $validPrice,
                            ]));
                            $stats['products_created']++;
                        }
                    }
                }, 1);
                file_put_contents($state, json_encode(['last_category' => $providerId, 'at' => date(DATE_ATOM), 'stats' => $stats]));
                if (($stats['fetched'] + $stats['cached']) % 20 === 0) {
                    $this->line('PROGRESS '.json_encode($stats));
                }
            }
            $complete = empty($queue) && $stats['failed'] === 0;
            $this->info('RESULT: '.($complete ? 'TREE_TRAVERSED' : 'PARTIAL_SYNC_WILL_RETRY'));
            $this->info('COUNTERS: '.json_encode($stats));
            $this->info('GLOBAL_MARKUP_PERCENT: '.$markup);
            $this->info('NO_PURCHASE_ATTEMPTED');
            return $complete ? self::SUCCESS : self::FAILURE;
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            Log::error('Oranos progressive sync failed', ['type'=>get_class($e), 'message'=>$e->getMessage()]);
            $this->error('SYNC_ERROR: '.get_class($e).' '.$e->getMessage());
            $this->info('COUNTERS: '.json_encode($stats));
            return self::FAILURE;
        } finally {
            flock($lock, LOCK_UN);
            fclose($lock);
        }
    }

    private function validContent($data): bool
    {
        return is_array($data) && is_array($data['categories'] ?? null) && is_array($data['products'] ?? null)
            && array_is_list($data['categories']) && array_is_list($data['products']);
    }
}


===== FILE: app\Console\Commands\SyncOranosVerified.php =====

<?php

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\Category;
use App\Models\Product;
use App\Models\Setting;
use App\Services\OranosMarketService;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

/** A complete provider snapshot must be fetched before any catalog row is changed. */
class SyncOranosVerified extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'oranos:sync-verified {--dry-run : Fetch and audit without DB writes} {--min-products=2395 : Abort if provider returns fewer products than this established baseline} {--max-seconds=21600 : Maximum time allowed for fetching}';
    protected $description = 'Stage the full Oranos category tree and products before an atomic, non-destructive catalog sync';

    public function handle(OranosMarketService $api): int
    {
        $begin = microtime(true);
        $deadline = $begin + max(60, (int) $this->option('max-seconds'));
        // Persist successfully fetched category responses across invocations.
        // Provider data is never committed to the database unless the entire tree passes validation.
        $cacheDir = storage_path('app/oranos-sync-checkpoints');
        if (!is_dir($cacheDir) && !@mkdir($cacheDir, 0755, true) && !is_dir($cacheDir)) {
            $this->error('Cannot create Oranos checkpoint directory');
            return self::FAILURE;
        }
        $lockHandle = fopen($cacheDir.'/sync.lock', 'c');
        if (!$lockHandle || !flock($lockHandle, LOCK_EX | LOCK_NB)) {
            $this->warn('RESULT: SYNC_ALREADY_RUNNING; not starting a second instance');
            return self::SUCCESS;
        }
        $cacheMaxAge = 24 * 3600; // bounded freshness window; never treat arbitrarily old content as current
        $nodes = [];
        $products = [];
        $queue = [[0, null]];
        $seen = [];
        $requests = 0;
        try {
            while ($queue) {
                if (microtime(true) >= $deadline) {
                    throw new \RuntimeException('Fetch deadline reached; nothing written.');
                }
                [$id, $parent] = array_shift($queue);
                if (isset($seen[$id])) continue;
                $seen[$id] = true;
                $content = null;
                $cachePath = $cacheDir.'/category-'.(int)$id.'.json';
                if (is_file($cachePath) && time() - filemtime($cachePath) <= $cacheMaxAge) {
                    $saved = json_decode((string) file_get_contents($cachePath), true);
                    if (is_array($saved) && is_array($saved['categories'] ?? null) && is_array($saved['products'] ?? null)) {
                        $content = $saved;
                    }
                }
                $attempt = 0;
                while ($content === null) {
                    if (microtime(true) >= $deadline) {
                        throw new \RuntimeException('Fetch time budget expired at category '.$id.'; checkpoint preserved; no database writes.');
                    }
                    $attempt++;
                    try {
                        $content = $api->getContent((int) $id);
                        if (!is_array($content['categories'] ?? null) || !is_array($content['products'] ?? null)) {
                            throw new \RuntimeException('Incomplete response at category '.$id.'; no database writes.');
                        }
                        // Atomic checkpoint write. Never cache transport errors or invalid shapes.
                        $encoded = json_encode($content, JSON_UNESCAPED_UNICODE | JSON_INVALID_UTF8_SUBSTITUTE);
                        if ($encoded === false) throw new \RuntimeException('Cannot serialize checkpoint for category '.$id);
                        $tmpPath = $cachePath.'.'.getmypid().'.tmp';
                        if (file_put_contents($tmpPath, $encoded, LOCK_EX) === false || !rename($tmpPath, $cachePath)) {
                            @unlink($tmpPath);
                            throw new \RuntimeException('Cannot save checkpoint for category '.$id);
                        }
                    } catch (\Illuminate\Http\Client\ConnectionException $e) {
                        $this->warn('NETWORK_RETRY category='.$id.' attempt='.$attempt.' elapsed='.round(microtime(true)-$begin).'s: '.substr($e->getMessage(), 0, 130));
                        $remaining = $deadline - microtime(true);
                        if ($remaining <= 0) break;
                        sleep((int) min($remaining, min(90, 10 + 5 * min($attempt, 16))));
                    }
                }
                if ($content === null) {
                    throw new \RuntimeException('Time budget reached at category '.$id.'; saved category checkpoints retained; no database writes.');
                }
                $requests++;
                if (!is_array($content['categories'] ?? null) || !is_array($content['products'] ?? null)) {
                    throw new \RuntimeException('Incomplete content response for Oranos category '.$id.'; no writes.');
                }
                foreach ($content['categories'] as $child) {
                    if (!is_array($child) || !is_numeric($child['id'] ?? null)) continue;
                    $childId = (int) $child['id'];
                    if ($childId <= 0 || $childId === (int) $id) continue;
                    $name = trim((string) ($child['name'] ?? ''));
                    if ($name === '' || strtolower($name) === 'null') {
                        // Cannot safely construct a public category from an unnamed node.
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (!isset($nodes[$childId])) {
                        $nodes[$childId] = ['name' => $name, 'parent' => $id === 0 ? null : (int) $id];
                        $queue[] = [$childId, $id];
                    }
                }
                foreach ($content['products'] as $product) {
                    if (!is_array($product) || !is_numeric($product['id'] ?? null)) continue;
                    $pid = (int) $product['id'];
                    if ($pid <= 0) continue;
                    // The source category is the content node, not a guessed name.
                    $sourceCat = is_numeric($product['parent_id'] ?? null) ? (int) $product['parent_id'] : (int) $id;
                    if ($sourceCat === 0 || !isset($nodes[$sourceCat])) $sourceCat = (int) $id;
                    if (isset($products[$pid]) && $products[$pid]['category'] !== $sourceCat) {
                        throw new \RuntimeException('Conflicting provider categories for product #'.$pid.'; no writes.');
                    }
                    $products[$pid] = ['category' => $sourceCat, 'data' => $product];
                }
                if ($requests % 30 === 0) {
                    $this->line("FETCHED nodes={$requests} products=".count($products));
                }
            }
            $minProducts = max(1, (int) $this->option('min-products'));
            $this->info('PROVIDER_ROOT_AND_CATEGORY_REQUESTS: '.$requests);
            $this->info('CHECKPOINT_WINDOW_HOURS: 24');
            $this->info('PROVIDER_NAMED_CATEGORIES: '.count($nodes));
            $this->info('PROVIDER_UNIQUE_PRODUCTS: '.count($products));
            if (count($products) < $minProducts) {
                throw new \RuntimeException('Incomplete provider snapshot: '.count($products).' products, required '.$minProducts.'. No writes.');
            }
            $missingRefs = 0;
            $badPrices = 0;
            foreach ($products as $entry) {
                if (!isset($nodes[$entry['category']])) $missingRefs++;
                if (!is_numeric($entry['data']['price'] ?? null) || (float) $entry['data']['price'] <= 0) $badPrices++;
            }
            $this->info('MISSING_PROVIDER_CATEGORY_REFERENCES: '.$missingRefs);
            $this->info('PROVIDER_INVALID_PRICES: '.$badPrices);
            if ($missingRefs) throw new \RuntimeException('Unresolved product category references. No writes.');
            if ($this->option('dry-run')) {
                $this->info('RESULT: VERIFIED_SNAPSHOT_READ_ONLY');
                return self::SUCCESS;
            }

            $markupPercent = (float) Setting::get('oranos_markup_percent', 20);
            if (!is_finite($markupPercent) || $markupPercent < 0 || $markupPercent > 10000) {
                throw new \RuntimeException('Invalid global Oranos markup setting; no writes.');
            }
            $counts = ['new_categories' => 0, 'new_products' => 0, 'updated_products' => 0];
            DB::transaction(function () use ($nodes, $products, $markupPercent, &$counts) {
                $categoryIds = [];
                // Resolve parents via rounds; provider tree must be acyclic and rooted.
                $remaining = $nodes;
                while ($remaining) {
                    $progress = false;
                    foreach ($remaining as $providerId => $info) {
                        $parentProvider = $info['parent'];
                        if ($parentProvider !== null && !isset($categoryIds[$parentProvider])) continue;
                        $parentLocal = $parentProvider === null ? null : $categoryIds[$parentProvider];
                        $category = Category::where('oranos_category_id', $providerId)->first();
                        if (!$category) {
                            $slug = Str::slug($info['name']).'-oranos-'.$providerId;
                            if (Str::slug($info['name']) === '') $slug = 'oranos-cat-'.$providerId;
                            $category = Category::create([
                                'oranos_category_id' => $providerId, 'parent_id' => $parentLocal,
                                'name' => $info['name'], 'name_ar' => $info['name'],
                                'slug' => $slug, 'type' => 'auto', 'icon' => 'package', 'sort_order' => 0,
                            ]);
                            $counts['new_categories']++;
                        } else {
                            // The provider owns structural placement, but not merchant labels or images.
                            if ($category->parent_id !== $parentLocal) $category->update(['parent_id' => $parentLocal]);
                        }
                        $categoryIds[$providerId] = $category->id;
                        unset($remaining[$providerId]);
                        $progress = true;
                    }
                    if (!$progress) throw new \RuntimeException('Category tree cycle/unresolvable parent. Rolled back.');
                }
                foreach ($products as $providerId => $entry) {
                    $data = $entry['data'];
                    $name = trim((string) ($data['name'] ?? ''));
                    if ($name === '') continue;
                    $price = is_numeric($data['price'] ?? null) ? (float) $data['price'] : 0.0;
                    $available = $data['available'] ?? true;
                    if (is_string($available)) $available = in_array(strtolower($available), ['active', 'available', 'in_stock', 'true', '1'], true);
                    $available = (bool) $available;
                    $retail = $price > 0 ? round($price * (1 + $markupPercent / 100), 4) : 0.0;
                    $existing = Product::where('oranos_product_id', $providerId)->first();
                    $payload = [
                        'category_id' => $categoryIds[$entry['category']],
                        'base_price' => $price,
                        'oranos_available' => $available,
                        'qty_values' => $data['qty_values'] ?? null,
                        'params' => is_array($data['params'] ?? null) ? $data['params'] : [],
                        'metadata' => array_merge(is_array($existing?->metadata) ? $existing->metadata : [], ['oranos_product_type' => $data['product_type'] ?? null]),
                    ];
                    if ($existing) {
                        if (($existing->pricing_mode ?? 'auto') === 'auto') $payload['price'] = $retail;
                        // Do not reactivate explicitly disabled merchant products.
                        if ($existing->is_active) $payload['is_active'] = $available && $price > 0;
                        $existing->update($payload);
                        $counts['updated_products']++;
                    } else {
                        $slug = Str::slug($name).'-oranos-'.$providerId;
                        if (Str::slug($name) === '') $slug = 'oranos-product-'.$providerId;
                        Product::create(array_merge($payload, [
                            'oranos_product_id' => $providerId,
                            'name' => $name, 'name_ar' => $name, 'description' => $name,
                            'description_ar' => $name, 'slug' => $slug, 'type' => 'auto',
                            'is_automation' => true, 'pricing_mode' => 'auto',
                            'price' => $retail, 'stock' => $available ? 999999999 : 0,
                            'is_active' => $available && $price > 0,
                        ]));
                        $counts['new_products']++;
                    }
                }
            }, 1);
            foreach ($counts as $key => $value) $this->info(strtoupper($key).': '.$value);
            $this->info('GLOBAL_MARKUP_PERCENT: '.$markupPercent);
            $this->info('RESULT: ATOMIC_SYNC_SUCCESS');
            return self::SUCCESS;
        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
            Log::error('Verified Oranos sync aborted', ['reason' => $e->getMessage()]);
            $this->error('RESULT: SYNC_ABORTED_OR_ROLLED_BACK');
            $this->error('REASON: '.$e->getMessage());
            return self::FAILURE;
        } finally {
            flock($lockHandle, LOCK_UN);
            fclose($lockHandle);
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: app\Console\Commands\SyncProductImagesFromCategories.php =====

<?php

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\Product;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;

class SyncProductImagesFromCategories extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'oranos:sync-product-images';
    protected $description = 'Sync product images from their category images';

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $updated = 0;
        $skipped = 0;
        $noCategoryImage = 0;

        Product::whereNull('image_url')
            ->orWhere('image_url', '')
            ->orWhere('image_url', 'https://api.oranosmarket.com/')
            ->chunk(500, function ($products) use (&$updated, &$skipped, &$noCategoryImage) {
                foreach ($products as $product) {
                    $category = $product->category;
                    if ($category && $category->image_url && $category->image_url !== '') {
                        $product->update(['image_url' => $category->image_url]);
                        $updated++;
                    } else {
                        $noCategoryImage++;
                    }
                }
            });

        // Also update products that have the placeholder
        Product::where('image_url', 'https://api.oranosmarket.com/')
            ->chunk(500, function ($products) use (&$updated, &$skipped, &$noCategoryImage) {
                foreach ($products as $product) {
                    $category = $product->category;
                    if ($category && $category->image_url && $category->image_url !== '') {
                        $product->update(['image_url' => $category->image_url]);
                        $updated++;
                    } else {
                        $noCategoryImage++;
                    }
                }
            });

        $this->newLine();
        $this->info("Updated: {$updated}. No category image: {$noCategoryImage}.");

        return 0;
    }
}


===== FILE: app\Console\Commands\VerifyOranosPriceSync.php =====

<?php

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Models\Product;
use App\Services\OranosMarketService;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;

class VerifyOranosPriceSync extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'oranos:verify-price-sync';
    protected $description = 'Verify Oranos product prices match our base_price';

    public function handle(OranosMarketService $service): int
    {
        $products = Product::whereNotNull('oranos_product_id')
            ->inRandomOrder()
            ->limit(10)
            ->get();

        if ($products->isEmpty()) {
            $this->warn('No Oranos-linked products found.');
            return 1;
        }

        $this->info('Fetching Oranos products...');
        $oranosProducts = $service->getProducts();
        $oranosMap = [];
        foreach ($oranosProducts as $op) {
            $oranosMap[(string)$op['id']] = $op;
        }

        $matches = 0;
        $mismatches = 0;

        foreach ($products as $product) {
            $oranosProduct = $oranosMap[(string)$product->oranos_product_id];
            if (!$oranosProduct) {
                $this->warn("[MISSING] {$product->slug} (oranos_id: {$product->oranos_product_id}) not found in Oranos");
                $mismatches++;
                continue;
            }

            $oranosPrice = (float)($oranosProduct['price'] ?? 0);
            $ourBasePrice = (float)$product->base_price;

            if (abs($oranosPrice - $ourBasePrice) < 0.01) {
                $this->info("[MATCH] {$product->slug}: Oranos={$oranosPrice} Ours={$ourBasePrice}");
                $matches++;
            } else {
                $this->error("[MISMATCH] {$product->slug}: Oranos={$oranosPrice} Ours={$ourBasePrice} Diff=" . abs($oranosPrice - $ourBasePrice));
                $mismatches++;
            }
        }

        $this->newLine();
        $this->info("Summary: {$matches} MATCH, {$mismatches} MISMATCH out of {$products->count()} checked.");

        return $mismatches > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    }
}

===== FILE: app\Enums\VipLevel.php =====

<?php

namespace App\Enums;

enum VipLevel: string
{
    case None = 'none';
    case Vip1 = 'vip1';
    case Vip2 = 'vip2';
    case Vip3 = 'vip3';
    case Vip4 = 'vip4';

    public function label(): string
    {
        return match ($this) {
            self::None => 'Regular',
            self::Vip1 => 'VIP 1',
            self::Vip2 => 'VIP 2',
            self::Vip3 => 'VIP 3',
            self::Vip4 => 'VIP 4 • Trader',
        };
    }

    public function defaultWithdrawalLimit(): float
    {
        return match ($this) {
            self::None => 0.0,
            self::Vip1 => 1000.0,
            self::Vip2 => 2000.0,
            self::Vip3, self::Vip4 => 0.0,
        };
    }

    public function defaultFeePercent(): float
    {
        return match ($this) {
            self::None => 5.0,
            self::Vip1 => 3.0,
            self::Vip2 => 1.5,
            self::Vip3, self::Vip4 => 0.0,
        };
    }
}


===== FILE: app\Events\DepositStatusChanged.php =====

<?php

namespace App\Events;

use App\Models\Transaction;
use Illuminate\Broadcasting\InteractsWithSockets;
use Illuminate\Broadcasting\PrivateChannel;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Broadcasting\ShouldBroadcastNow;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Events\Dispatchable;
use Illuminate\Queue\SerializesModels;

class DepositStatusChanged implements ShouldBroadcastNow
{
    use Dispatchable, InteractsWithSockets, SerializesModels;

    public function __construct(public Transaction $transaction)
    {
    }

    public function broadcastOn(): PrivateChannel
    {
        return new PrivateChannel('user.' . $this->transaction->user_id);
    }

    public function broadcastAs(): string
    {
        return 'deposit.status';
    }

    public function broadcastWith(): array
    {
        return [
            'transaction_id' => $this->transaction->id,
            'status' => $this->transaction->status->value,
            'amount' => (float) $this->transaction->amount,
        ];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Events;

use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Broadcasting\InteractsWithSockets;
use Illuminate\Broadcasting\PrivateChannel;
use Illuminate\Contracts\Broadcasting\ShouldBroadcastNow;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Events\Dispatchable;
use Illuminate\Queue\SerializesModels;

class VipLevelChanged implements ShouldBroadcastNow
{
    use Dispatchable, InteractsWithSockets, SerializesModels;

    public function __construct(public User $user, public string $oldLevel, public string $newLevel)
    {
    }

    public function broadcastOn(): PrivateChannel
    {
        return new PrivateChannel('user.' . $this->user->id);

    public function broadcastAs(): string
    {
